> DEVELOPER

Investigate GitHub issue #408: "0.9.5: scan/status/next crash in fresh install (export + missing key)"

Two bugs reported:

1. `AttributeError: module 'desloppify.engine.planning' has no attribute 'generate_issues'` — The function `generate_issues` exists in `desloppify.engine.planning.scan` but is not re-exported from `desloppify/engine/planning/__init__.py`. The caller is `app/commands/scan/workflow.py` which does `import desloppify.engine.planning as plan_mod` then `plan_mod.generate_issues(...)`.

2. `KeyError: 'failing'` in `desloppify/engine/_scoring/results/impact.py` — assumes every detector row has a `failing` key.

For each bug:
- Find the exact lines of code involved
- Determine if the bug is real and present on the current branch
- Assess how easy it is to fix

This is research only — do NOT make any code changes.

```
1→"""Issue generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import rel
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.base.discovery.paths import get_project_root
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import is_subjective_phase
    13→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    14→from desloppify.languages.framework import (
    15→    DetectorPhase,
    16→    LangConfig,
    17→    LangRun,
    18→    auto_detect_lang,
    19→    available_langs,
    20→    capability_report,
    21→    get_lang,
    22→    make_lang_run,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.state_io import Issue
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class PlanScanOptions:
    29→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    30→
    31→    include_slow: bool = True
    32→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    33→    profile: str = "full"
    34→
    35→
    36→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    37→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    38→
    39→
    40→def _resolve_lang(
    41→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    42→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    43→    if lang is not None:
    44→        return lang
    45→
    46→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    47→    if detected is None:
    48→        langs = available_langs()
    49→        if not langs:
    50→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    51→        detected = langs[0]
    52→    return get_lang(detected)
    53→
    54→
    55→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    56→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    57→        return
    58→
    59→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    60→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    61→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    62→    )
    63→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    64→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    65→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    66→    )
    67→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    68→
    69→    report = capability_report(lang)
    70→    if report is not None:
    71→        present, missing = report
    72→        if present:
    73→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    74→        if missing:
    75→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    76→
    77→
    78→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    79→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    80→    phases = lang.phases
    81→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    82→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    83→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    84→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    85→    return phases
    86→
    87→
    88→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
    89→    issues: list[Issue] = []
    90→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    91→
    92→    total = len(phases)
    93→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    94→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    95→        phase_issues, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    96→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    97→        issues.extend(phase_issues)
    98→
    99→    return issues, all_potentials
   100→
   101→
   102→def _stamp_issue_context(issues: list[Issue], lang: LangRun) -> None:
   103→    if not issues:
   104→        return
   105→
   106→    zone_policies = None
   107→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   108→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   109→
   110→    for issue in issues:
   111→        issue["lang"] = lang.name
   112→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(issue.get("file", ""))
   116→        issue["zone"] = zone.value
   117→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   118→        if policy and issue.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   119→            issue["confidence"] = "low"
   120→
   121→
   122→def _generate_issues_from_lang(
   123→    path: Path,
   124→    lang: LangRun,
   125→    *,
   126→    include_slow: bool = True,
   127→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   128→    profile: str = "full",
   129→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   130→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   131→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   132→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   133→    issues, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   134→    _stamp_issue_context(issues, lang)
   135→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(issues)} issues")
   136→    return issues, all_potentials
   137→
   138→
   139→def generate_issues(
   140→    path: Path,
   141→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   142→    *,
   143→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   144→) -> tuple[list[Issue], dict[str, int]]:
   145→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized issues."""
   146→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   147→
   148→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, get_project_root())
   149→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   150→    return _generate_issues_from_lang(
   151→        path,
   152→        runtime_lang,
   153→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   154→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   155→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
   156→    )
   157→
```

```
    77→def _clear_needs_rescan_flag(config: dict[str, object]) -> None:

   179→def _configure_lang_runtime(

   208→    }
   209→    return runtime_lang

   211→
   212→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   213→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   214→
   215→    Sets score to 0.0, stamps assessed_at/reset_by/placeholder, and strips
   216→    any cached scoring artifacts (integrity_penalty, components,
   217→    component_scores) plus provisional-override markers.
   218→    """
   219→    payload["score"] = 0.0
   220→    payload["source"] = source
   221→    payload["assessed_at"] = now
   222→    payload["reset_by"] = source
   223→    payload["placeholder"] = True
   224→    payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   225→    payload.pop("components", None)
   226→    payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   227→    payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   228→    payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   229→
   230→
   231→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   232→    state: StateModel,
   233→    *,
   234→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   235→) -> int:
   236→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   237→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   238→
   239→    reset_keys = {
   240→        key.strip()
   241→        for key in assessments
   242→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   243→    }
   244→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   245→
   246→    now = utc_now()
   247→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   248→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   249→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   250→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   251→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   252→            continue
   253→        assessments[key] = {
   254→            "score": 0.0,
   255→            "source": source,
   256→            "assessed_at": now,
   257→            "reset_by": source,
   258→            "placeholder": True,
   259→        }
   260→    return len(reset_keys)
   261→
   262→
   263→
   264→
   265→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   266→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   267→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   268→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   269→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   270→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   271→    path = Path(args.path)
   272→    reset_script_import_caches(str(path))
   273→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   274→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   275→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   276→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   277→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   278→            state,
   279→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   283→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   284→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   285→
   286→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   287→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   288→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   289→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   290→
   291→    return ScanRuntime(
   292→        args=args,
   293→        state_path=state_file,
   294→        state=state,
   295→        path=path,
   296→        config=config,
   297→        lang=lang,
   298→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   299→        profile=profile,
   300→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   301→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   302→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   303→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   304→        force_rescan=bool(getattr(args, "force_rescan", False)),
   305→    )
   306→
   307→
   308→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   309→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   310→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   311→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   312→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   313→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   314→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   315→        return issues
   316→
   317→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   318→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   319→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   320→    )
   321→    if not stale:
   322→        return issues
   323→
   324→    augmented = list(issues)
   325→    augmented.extend(stale)
   326→    for stale_issue in stale:
   327→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   328→    return augmented
   329→
   330→
   331→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   332→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   333→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   334→    *,
   335→    decay_scans: int,
   336→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   337→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   338→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   339→        issues,
   340→        state=runtime.state,
   341→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   342→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   343→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   344→    )
   345→
   346→
   347→def run_scan_generation(
   348→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   349→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   350→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   351→    enable_file_cache()
   352→    enable_parse_cache()
   353→    try:
   354→        issues, potentials = generate_plan_issues(
   355→            runtime.path,
   356→            lang=runtime.lang,
   357→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   358→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   359→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   360→                profile=runtime.profile,
   361→            ),
   362→        )
   363→    finally:
   364→        disable_parse_cache()
   365→        disable_file_cache()
   366→
   367→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   368→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   369→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   370→    )
   371→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   372→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   373→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   374→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   375→    )
   376→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   377→        issues,
   378→        runtime,
   379→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   380→    )
   381→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   382→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   383→
   384→
   385→def merge_scan_results(
   386→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   387→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   388→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   389→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   390→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   391→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   392→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   393→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   394→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   395→
   396→    if not path_changed:
   397→        prev = score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   398→    else:
   399→        prev = ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   400→    prev_dim_scores = (
   401→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   402→    )
   403→
   404→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   405→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   406→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   407→
   408→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   409→
   410→    diff = merge_scan(
   411→        runtime.state,
   412→        issues,
   413→        options=MergeScanOptions(
   414→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   415→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   416→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   417→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   418→            potentials=potentials,
   419→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   420→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   421→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   422→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   423→        ),
   424→    )
   425→
   426→    mark_stale_holistic(
   427→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   428→    )
   429→    save_state(
   430→        runtime.state,
   431→        runtime.state_path,
   432→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   433→    )
   434→
   435→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   436→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   437→
   438→    return ScanMergeResult(
   439→        diff=diff,
   440→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   441→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   442→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   443→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   444→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   445→    )
   446→
   447→
   448→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   449→    state: StateModel,
   450→    config: dict[str, object],
   451→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   452→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden issue counters."""
   453→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   454→        resolve_issue_noise_settings(config)
   455→    )
   456→    issues_by_id = _state_issues(state)
   457→    open_issues = [
   458→        issue
   459→        for issue in path_scoped_issues(
   460→            issues_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   461→        ).values()
   462→        if issue.get("status") == "open"
   463→    ]
   464→    _, hidden_by_detector = apply_issue_noise_budget(
   465→        open_issues,
   466→        budget=noise_budget,
   467→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   468→    )
   469→
   470→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   471→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   472→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   473→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   474→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   475→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   476→    )
   477→
   478→
   479→def persist_reminder_history(
   480→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   481→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   482→) -> None:
   483→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   484→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   485→        return
   486→
   487→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   488→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   489→    save_state(
   490→        runtime.state,
   491→        runtime.state_path,
   492→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   493→    )
   494→
   495→
   496→__all__ = [
   497→    "ScanStateContractError",
   498→    "ScanMergeResult",
   499→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   500→    "ScanRuntime",
   501→    "merge_scan_results",
   502→    "persist_reminder_history",
   503→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   504→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   505→    "run_scan_generation",
   506→]
   507→
```

```
1→"""Detector-to-score impact helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     6→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
     9→    Dimension,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_score
    12→
    13→
    14→def compute_score_impact(
    15→    dimension_scores: dict,
    16→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    17→    detector: str,
    18→    issues_to_fix: int,
    19→) -> float:
    20→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
    21→    target_dim = None
    22→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    23→        if detector in dim.detectors:
    24→            target_dim = dim
    25→            break
    26→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
    27→        return 0.0
    28→
    29→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    30→    if potential <= 0:
    31→        return 0.0
    32→
    33→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
    34→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
    35→
    36→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
    37→    if not det_data:
    38→        return 0.0
    39→
    40→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
    41→    avg_weight = old_weighted / max(1, det_data["failing"])
    42→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * avg_weight)
    43→
    44→    total_potential = 0
    45→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
    46→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
    47→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
    48→        if not det_values:
    49→            continue
    50→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
    51→        if det == detector:
    52→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
    53→        else:
    54→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
    55→    if total_potential <= 0:
    56→        return 0.0
    57→
    58→    new_dim_score = (
    59→        max(
    60→            0.0,
    61→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
    62→        )
    63→        * 100
    64→    )
    65→
    66→    simulated = {name: dict(data) for name, data in dimension_scores.items()}
    67→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
    68→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
    69→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
    70→
    71→
    72→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
    73→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
    74→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
    75→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
    76→        return None
    77→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
    78→
    79→
    80→__all__ = ["compute_score_impact", "get_dimension_for_detector"]
    81→
    82→
```

```
1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.issue_id_display import short_issue_id
     9→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    13→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    14→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    15→    TriageSnapshot,
    16→    build_triage_snapshot,
    17→    triage_phase_banner,
    18→)
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass
    24→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    25→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    26→
    27→    is_stale: bool = False
    28→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    29→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    30→    _snapshot: TriageSnapshot | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    31→
    32→
    33→def triage_guardrail_status(
    34→    *,
    35→    plan: dict | None = None,
    36→    state: dict | None = None,
    37→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    38→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    39→    try:
    40→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    41→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    42→        logger.debug("Triage guardrail status skipped: plan could not be loaded.", exc_info=exc)
    43→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    44→
    45→    resolved_state = state or {}
    46→
    47→    snapshot = build_triage_snapshot(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    48→    if not snapshot.is_triage_stale:
    49→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan, _snapshot=snapshot)
    50→
    51→    return TriageGuardrailResult(
    52→        is_stale=True,
    53→        new_ids=set(snapshot.new_since_triage_ids),
    54→        _plan=resolved_plan,
    55→        _snapshot=snapshot,
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def triage_guardrail_messages(
    60→    *,
    61→    plan: dict | None = None,
    62→    state: dict | None = None,
    63→) -> list[str]:
    64→    """Return warning strings without printing."""
    65→    resolved_state = state or {}
    66→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    67→    if not result.is_stale:
    68→        return []
    69→
    70→    messages: list[str] = []
    71→    if result.new_ids:
    72→        messages.append(
    73→            f"{len(result.new_ids)} new review issue(s) not yet triaged."
    74→            " Run the staged triage runner to incorporate them "
    75→            f"(`{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}` or `{TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}`)."
    76→        )
    77→
    78→    if result._plan is not None:
    79→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan, resolved_state, snapshot=result._snapshot)
    80→        if banner:
    81→            messages.append(banner)
    82→
    83→    return messages
    84→
    85→
    86→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    87→    *,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→    state: dict | None = None,
    90→) -> bool:
    91→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    92→    messages = triage_guardrail_messages(plan=plan, state=state)
    93→    for msg in messages:
    94→        print(colorize(f"  {msg}", "yellow"))
    95→    return bool(messages)
    96→
    97→
    98→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    99→    *,
   100→    state: dict,
   101→    bypass: bool = False,
   102→    attest: str = "",
   103→) -> None:
   104→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
   105→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
   106→    if not result.is_stale:
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
   110→        print(colorize(
   111→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
   112→            "yellow",
   113→        ))
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    new_ids = result.new_ids
   117→    lines = [
   118→        f"BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review issue(s) have not been triaged."
   119→    ]
   120→    if new_ids:
   121→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   122→            issue = state.get("issues", {}).get(fid, {})
   123→            lines.append(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {issue.get('summary', '')}")
   124→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   125→            lines.append(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more")
   126→    lines.append("")
   127→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Codex):  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}")
   128→    lines.append(f"  NEXT STEP (Claude): {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}")
   129→    lines.append("  Manual fallback:    desloppify plan triage")
   130→    lines.append("  (Review new issues, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)")
   131→    lines.append("")
   132→    lines.append("  View new execution items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent")
   133→    lines.append("  View broader backlog:      desloppify backlog")
   134→    lines.append('  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."')
   135→    raise CommandError("\n".join(lines))
   136→
   137→
   138→__all__ = [
   139→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   140→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   141→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   142→    "triage_guardrail_messages",
   143→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
    33→from .selection import (

    38→)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _validate_fixed_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> bool:
    42→    if args.status != "fixed":
    43→        return True
    44→    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    45→    if validate_note_length(note):
    46→        return True
    47→    show_note_length_requirement(note)
    48→    return False
    49→
    50→
    51→def _load_state_with_guards(
    52→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    53→    *,
    54→    attestation: str | None,
    55→) -> tuple[str, dict, object] | None:
    56→    """Validate inputs, apply guardrails, and load state for resolve."""
    57→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    58→    if not _validate_fixed_note(args):
    59→        return None
    60→
    61→    state_file = state_path(args)
    62→    state = load_state(state_file)
    63→    plan_access = load_resolve_plan_access()
    64→
    65→    if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
    66→        if _check_queue_order_guard(
    67→            state,
    68→            args.patterns,
    69→            args.status,
    70→            plan_access=plan_access,
    71→        ):
    72→            return None
    73→
    74→    if args.status == "fixed":
    75→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
    76→            state=state,
    77→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    78→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    return state_file, state, plan_access
    82→
    83→
    84→def _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
    85→    state: dict,
    86→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    87→    *,
    88→    attestation: str | None,
    89→    plan_access,
    90→) -> tuple[object, dict[str, float], list[str]]:
    91→    """Apply resolve patterns and return pre/post context for rendering."""
    92→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    93→        state,
    94→        args,
    95→        attestation=attestation,
    96→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    97→    )
    98→
    99→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
   100→    prev_subjective_scores = {
   101→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
   102→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
   103→        if isinstance(dim, str)
   104→    }
   105→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(
   106→        state,
   107→        args,
   108→        attestation=attestation,
   109→        plan_access=plan_access,
   110→    )
   111→    return prev, prev_subjective_scores, all_resolved
   112→
   113→
   114→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   115→    """Resolve issue(s) matching one or more patterns."""
   116→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   117→    loaded = _load_state_with_guards(args, attestation=attestation)
   118→    if loaded is None:
   119→        return
   120→    state_file, state, plan_access = loaded
   121→    prev, prev_subjective_scores, all_resolved = _resolve_ids_with_snapshots(
   122→        state,
   123→        args,
   124→        attestation=attestation,
   125→        plan_access=plan_access,
   126→    )
   127→    if not all_resolved:
   128→        print_no_match_warning(args)
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   132→
   133→    plan, cluster_ctx = update_living_plan_after_resolve(
   134→        args=args,
   135→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   136→        attestation=attestation,
   137→        state_file=state_file,
   138→    )
   139→    mid_cluster = (
   140→        cluster_ctx.cluster_name is not None and not cluster_ctx.cluster_completed
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   144→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   145→        state,
   146→        status=args.status,
   147→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   148→    )
   149→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   150→    if not mid_cluster:
   151→        render_commit_guidance(state, plan, all_resolved, args.status)
   152→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   153→        args=args,
   154→        state=state,
   155→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   156→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   157→    )
   158→
   159→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   160→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   161→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   162→        state,
   163→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   164→    )
   165→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   166→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   167→
   168→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   169→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   170→        ResolveQueryContext(
   171→            patterns=args.patterns,
   172→            status=args.status,
   173→            resolved=all_resolved,
   174→            next_command=next_command,
   175→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   176→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   177→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   178→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   179→            attestation=attestation,
   180→            narrative=narrative,
   181→            state=state,
   182→        )
   183→    )
   184→    print_fixed_next_user_message(
   185→        args=args,
   186→        plan=plan,
   187→        next_command=next_command,
   188→        mid_cluster=mid_cluster,
   189→        cluster_ctx=cluster_ctx,
   190→    )
   191→
   192→
   193→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_resolve"]
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import logging
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import (
    10→    write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort,
    11→)
    12→
    13→from .plan_load import ResolvePlanAccess, load_resolve_plan_access
    14→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    15→
    16→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _try_expand_cluster(
    20→    pattern: str,
    21→    *,
    22→    plan_access: ResolvePlanAccess | None = None,
    23→) -> list[str] | None:
    24→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its issue IDs."""
    25→    resolved_plan_access = plan_access or load_resolve_plan_access()
    26→    plan = resolved_plan_access.usable_plan(
    27→        behavior="Cluster-name pattern expansion is disabled until plan loading succeeds.",
    28→    )
    29→    if plan is None:
    30→        _logger.debug(
    31→            "cluster expansion skipped for %r because no usable living plan is available",
    32→            pattern,
    33→        )
    34→        return None
    35→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    36→    if cluster and cluster.get("issue_ids"):
    37→        return list(cluster["issue_ids"])
    38→    return None
    39→
    40→
    41→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    42→    state: dict,
    43→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    44→    *,
    45→    attestation: str | None,
    46→    plan_access: ResolvePlanAccess | None = None,
    47→) -> list[str]:
    48→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    49→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    50→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    51→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern, plan_access=plan_access)
    52→        if cluster_ids:
    53→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    54→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    55→                    state,
    56→                    fid,
    57→                    args.status,
    58→                    args.note,
    59→                    attestation=attestation,
    60→                )
    61→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    62→            continue
    63→
    64→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_issues(
    65→            state,
    66→            pattern,
    67→            args.status,
    68→            args.note,
    69→            attestation=attestation,
    70→        )
    71→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    72→    return all_resolved
    73→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    74→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    75→    _write_query_best_effort(
    76→        {
    77→            "command": "resolve",
    78→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    79→            "status": context.status,
    80→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    81→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    82→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    83→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    84→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    85→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    86→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    87→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    88→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    89→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    90→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    91→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    92→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    93→        },
    94→        context="resolve query payload update",
    95→    )
    96→
```

```
    26→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    40→
```

```
    14→class TestPlanModuleSanity:
    15→    """Verify the module imports and has expected exports."""
    16→
    17→    def test_cmd_plan_output_callable(self):
    18→        assert callable(cmd_plan_output)
    19→
    20→    def test_cmd_plan_output_signature(self):
```

```
    21→def cmd_autofix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    23→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    24→
    25→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    26→    path = Path(args.path)
    27→
    28→    lang, fixer = resolve_fixer_config(args, fixer_name)
    29→
    30→    if not dry_run:
    31→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    32→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    33→    if not entries:
    34→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    35→        return
    36→
    37→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    38→    results = raw.entries
    39→    skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    40→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    41→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    42→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    43→
    44→    if dry_run and results:
    45→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    46→
    47→    if not dry_run:
    48→        _apply_and_report(
    49→            args,
    50→            path,
    51→            fixer,
    52→            fixer_name,
    53→            entries,
    54→            results,
    55→            total_items,
    56→            lang,
    57→            skip_reasons,
    58→        )
    59→    else:
    60→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    61→    print()
```

```
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import argparse
     6→import sys

     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context

    19→
    20→from .apply_retro import (
    21→    _cascade_unused_import_cleanup,
    22→    _SKIP_REASON_LABELS,
    23→    _print_fix_retro,
    24→    _resolve_fixer_results,
    25→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    26→)
    27→from .fixer_selection import _COMMAND_POST_FIX
    28→
    29→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    30→    from desloppify.languages.framework import FixerConfig, LangRun
    31→
    32→
    33→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    34→    print(colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer.label}...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    35→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    36→    file_count = len(set(entry["file"] for entry in entries))
    37→    print(
    38→        colorize(
    39→            f"  Found {len(entries)} {fixer.label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim"
    40→        ),
    41→        file=sys.stderr,
    42→    )
    43→    return entries
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_fix_summary(
    47→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    48→    results: list[dict],
    49→    total_items: int,
    50→    total_lines: int,
    51→    dry_run: bool,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    54→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        colorize(
    57→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    58→            "bold",
    59→        )
    60→    )
    61→    for result in results[:30]:
    62→        symbols = ", ".join(result["removed"][:5])
    63→        if len(result["removed"]) > 5:
    64→            symbols += f" (+{len(result['removed']) - 5})"
    65→        extra = f"  ({result['lines_removed']} lines)" if result.get("lines_removed") else ""
    66→        print(f"  {rel(result['file'])}{extra}  →  {symbols}")
    67→    if len(results) > 30:
    68→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    69→
    70→
    71→def _apply_and_report(
    72→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    73→    path: Path,
    74→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    75→    fixer_name: str,
    76→    entries: list[dict],
    77→    results: list[dict],
    78→    total_items: int,
    79→    lang: LangRun | None,
    80→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
    81→) -> None:
    82→    state_file = state_path(args)
    83→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    84→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    85→    resolved_ids = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, fixer.detector, fixer_name)
    86→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    87→
    88→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    89→    print(f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} issues in state")
    90→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
    91→
    92→    if fixer.post_fix:
    93→        fixer.post_fix(path, state, prev.overall or 0, False, lang=lang)
    94→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    95→
    96→    if skip_reasons is None:
    97→        skip_reasons = {}
    98→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
    99→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   100→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   101→        state,
   102→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="autofix"),
   103→    )
   104→    typecheck_cmd = getattr(lang, "typecheck_cmd", "")
   105→    if typecheck_cmd:
   106→        next_action = f"Run `{typecheck_cmd}` to verify, then `desloppify scan` to update state"
   107→    else:
   108→        next_action = "Run `desloppify scan` to update state"
   109→
   110→    write_query(
   111→        {
   112→            "command": "autofix",
   113→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   114→            "files_fixed": len(results),
   115→            "items_fixed": total_items,
   116→            "issues_resolved": len(resolved_ids),
   117→            "overall_score": new.overall,
   118→            "objective_score": new.objective,
   119→            "strict_score": new.strict,
   120→            "prev_overall_score": prev.overall,
   121→            "prev_objective_score": prev.objective,
   122→            "prev_strict_score": prev.strict,
   123→            "skip_reasons": skip_reasons,
   124→            "next_action": next_action,
   125→            "narrative": narrative,
   126→        }
   127→    )
   128→    _print_fix_retro(fixer_name, len(entries), total_items, len(resolved_ids), skip_reasons)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _report_dry_run(
   132→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   133→    fixer_name: str,
   134→    entries: list[dict],
   135→    results: list[dict],
   136→    total_items: int,
   137→) -> None:
   138→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   139→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   140→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   141→    state = runtime.state
   142→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   143→        state,
   144→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="autofix"),
   145→    )
   146→    write_query(
   147→        {
   148→            "command": "autofix",
   149→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   150→            "dry_run": True,
   151→            "files_would_fix": len(results),
   152→            "items_would_fix": total_items,
   153→            "narrative": narrative,
   154→        }
   155→    )
   156→
   157→    skipped = len(entries) - total_items
   158→    if skipped > 0:
   159→        print(colorize("\n  ── Review ──", "dim"))
   160→        print(
   161→            colorize(
   162→                f"  {total_items} of {len(entries)} entries would be fixed ({skipped} skipped).",
   163→                "dim",
   164→            )
   165→        )
   166→        for question in [
   167→            "Do the sample changes look correct? Any false positives?",
   168→            "Are the skipped items truly unfixable, or could the fixer be improved?",
   169→            "Ready to run without --dry-run? (git push first!)",
   170→        ]:
   171→            print(colorize(f"  - {question}", "dim"))
   172→
   173→
   174→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   175→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["dead-useeffect"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   176→
   177→
   178→__all__ = [

   180→    "_apply_and_report",
   181→    "_detect",
   182→    "_print_fix_summary",
   183→    "_print_fix_retro",
   184→    "_report_dry_run",
   185→    "_resolve_fixer_results",
   186→    "_warn_uncommitted_changes",
   187→]
   188→
```

```
1→"""Shared prompt rendering sections used by both batch and external review paths."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.intelligence.review.feedback_contract import (
     9→    max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count,
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→class PromptBatchPayload(TypedDict, total=False):
    14→    """Typed packet batch contract used by prompt rendering."""
    15→
    16→    name: str
    17→    dimensions: list[str]
    18→    why: str
    19→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]]
    20→    judgment_finding_counts: dict[str, object]
    21→    mechanical_finding_counts: dict[str, object]
    22→    concern_signals: list[dict[str, object]]
    23→    historical_issue_focus: dict[str, object]
    24→    subjective_defer_meta: dict[str, dict[str, object]]
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    28→class PromptBatchContext:
    29→    name: str
    30→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...]
    31→    rationale: str
    32→    issues_cap: int
    33→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]]
    34→
    35→    @property
    36→    def dimension_set(self) -> set[str]:
    37→        return set(self.dimensions)
    38→
    39→    @property
    40→    def dimensions_text(self) -> str:
    41→        return ", ".join(self.dimensions) if self.dimensions else "(none)"
    42→
    43→
    44→def coerce_string_list(raw: object) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, list | tuple):
    46→        return ()
    47→    return tuple(str(item) for item in raw if isinstance(item, str) and item)
    48→
    49→
    50→def build_batch_context(batch: PromptBatchPayload, batch_index: int) -> PromptBatchContext:
    51→    dimensions = coerce_string_list(batch.get("dimensions", []))
    52→    return PromptBatchContext(
    53→        name=str(batch.get("name", f"Batch {batch_index + 1}")),
    54→        dimensions=dimensions,
    55→        rationale=str(batch.get("why", "")).strip(),
    56→        issues_cap=max_batch_issues_for_dimension_count(len(dimensions)),
    57→        dimension_prompts=batch_dimension_prompts(batch),
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def batch_dimension_prompts(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    62→    raw_prompts = batch.get("dimension_prompts")
    63→    if not isinstance(raw_prompts, dict):
    64→        return {}
    65→    return {
    66→        str(dim): prompt
    67→        for dim, prompt in raw_prompts.items()
    68→        if isinstance(dim, str) and isinstance(prompt, dict)
    69→    }
    70→
    71→
    72→SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION = {
    73→    "initialization_coupling": (

    77→        "dependencies, coupling through shared mutable state, and whether state should "
    78→        "be encapsulated behind a proper registry/context manager.\n"
    79→    ),
    80→    "design_coherence": (

    83→        "multiple mechanical detectors fired. Investigate what design change would address "
    84→        "multiple signals simultaneously. Check `scan_evidence.complexity_hotspots` for "
    85→        "files with high responsibility cluster counts.\n"
    86→    ),
    87→    "error_consistency": (
    88→        "9g. For error_consistency, use evidence from "
    89→        "`holistic_context.errors.exception_hotspots` — files with "
    90→        "concentrated exception handling issues. Investigate whether error handling is "
    91→        "designed or accidental. Check for broad catches masking specific failure modes.\n"
    92→    ),
    93→    "cross_module_architecture": (
    94→        "9h. For cross_module_architecture, also consult "
    95→        "`holistic_context.coupling.boundary_violations` for import paths that "
    96→        "cross architectural boundaries, and `holistic_context.dependencies.deferred_import_density` "
    97→        "for files with many function-level imports (proxy for cycle pressure).\n"
    98→    ),
    99→    "convention_outlier": (
   100→        "9i. For convention_outlier, also consult "
   101→        "`holistic_context.conventions.duplicate_clusters` for cross-file "
   102→        "function duplication and `conventions.naming_drift` for directory-level naming "
   103→        "inconsistency.\n"
   104→    ),
   105→}
   106→
   107→
   108→def render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   109→    """Render dimension-specific scan_evidence guidance."""
   110→    return "".join(
   111→        text
   112→        for dim, text in SCAN_EVIDENCE_FOCUS_BY_DIMENSION.items()
   113→        if dim in dim_set
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→_HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS = (
   118→    ("open", "Still open"),
   119→    ("deferred", "Deferred"),
   120→    ("triaged_out", "Triaged out"),
   121→)
   122→_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_GROUP = "Resolved"
   123→_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES = {"fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved"}
   124→
   125→
   126→def render_historical_focus(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:
   127→    focus = batch.get("historical_issue_focus")
   128→    if not isinstance(focus, dict):
   129→        return ""
   130→
   131→    selected_raw = focus.get("selected_count", 0)
   132→    try:
   133→        selected_count = max(0, int(selected_raw))
   134→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   135→        selected_count = 0
   136→
   137→    issues = focus.get("issues", [])
   138→    if not isinstance(issues, list):
   139→        issues = []
   140→
   141→    if selected_count <= 0 or not issues:
   142→        return ""
   143→
   144→    lines: list[str] = [
   145→        "Previously flagged issues — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:",
   146→        "Check whether open issues still exist. Do not re-report resolved or deferred items.",
   147→        "If several past issues share a root cause, call that out.",
   148→    ]
   149→
   150→    # Group issues by status category
   151→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   152→    for entry in issues:
   153→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   154→            continue
   155→        status = str(entry.get("status", "open")).strip()
   156→        grouped.setdefault(status, []).append(entry)
   157→
   158→    def _render_entry(entry: dict) -> str:
   159→        status = str(entry.get("status", "")).strip()
   160→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   161→        note = str(entry.get("note", "")).strip()
   162→        prefix = f"[{status}] " if status else ""
   163→        line = f"    - {prefix}{summary}"
   164→        if note:
   165→            line += f" (note: {note})"
   166→        return line
   167→
   168→    # Render active groups first (open, deferred, triaged_out)
   169→    for status_key, group_label in _HISTORICAL_STATUS_GROUPS:
   170→        group = grouped.pop(status_key, [])
   171→        if group:
   172→            lines.append(f"\n  {group_label} ({len(group)}):")
   173→            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)
   174→
   175→    # Render resolved group (all remaining resolved statuses)
   176→    resolved: list[dict] = []
   177→    for status_key in list(grouped):
   178→        if status_key in _HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_STATUSES:
   179→            resolved.extend(grouped.pop(status_key))
   180→    if resolved:
   181→        lines.append(f"\n  {_HISTORICAL_RESOLVED_GROUP} ({len(resolved)}):")
   182→        lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in resolved)
   183→
   184→    # Any unknown statuses
   185→    for status_key, group in grouped.items():
   186→        if group:
   187→            lines.append(f"\n  {status_key} ({len(group)}):")
   188→            lines.extend(_render_entry(e) for e in group)
   189→
   190→    lines.append("")
   191→    lines.append("Explore past review issues:")
   192→    lines.append("  desloppify show review --no-budget              # all open review issues")
   193→    lines.append("  desloppify show review --status deferred         # deferred issues")
   194→
   195→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   196→
   197→
   198→def render_dimension_deferral_context(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:
   199→    """Render deferral context for dimensions that were deferred for multiple cycles."""
   200→    defer_meta = batch.get("subjective_defer_meta")
   201→    if not isinstance(defer_meta, dict) or not defer_meta:
   202→        return ""
   203→
   204→    lines: list[str] = []
   205→    for dim, meta in defer_meta.items():
   206→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   207→            continue
   208→        cycles = meta.get("deferred_cycles", 0)
   209→        if not isinstance(cycles, int) or cycles < 1:
   210→            continue
   211→        lines.append(
   212→            f"Note: {dim} was deferred for {cycles} scan cycle(s) while objective issues took priority."
   213→        )
   214→        lines.append(
   215→            "Previous assessment may be stale — calibrate accordingly."
   216→        )
   217→    if not lines:
   218→        return ""
   219→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   220→
   221→
   222→def _concern_signal_lines(entry: dict[str, object]) -> list[str]:
   223→    """Render one concern signal entry into prompt lines."""
   224→    file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown file)"
   225→    concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   226→    summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
   227→    question = str(entry.get("question", "")).strip()
   228→    evidence_raw = entry.get("evidence", [])
   229→    evidence = (
   230→        [str(item).strip() for item in evidence_raw if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()]
   231→        if isinstance(evidence_raw, list)
   232→        else []
   233→    )
   234→    lines = [f"  - [{concern_type}] {file}"]
   235→    if summary:
   236→        lines.append(f"    summary: {summary}")
   237→    if question:
   238→        lines.append(f"    question: {question}")
   239→    lines.extend(f"    evidence: {snippet}" for snippet in evidence[:2])
   240→    fingerprint = str(entry.get("fingerprint", "")).strip()
   241→    if fingerprint:
   242→        lines.append(f"    fingerprint: {fingerprint}")
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _iter_valid_concern_signals(
   247→    signals: list[object],
   248→) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
   249→    """Filter signal entries to mapping payloads only."""
   250→    return [entry for entry in signals if isinstance(entry, dict)]
   251→
   252→
   253→def _build_concern_summary(valid_signals: list[dict[str, object]]) -> list[str]:
   254→    """Build a grouped summary of concern signals by type."""
   255→    by_type: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   256→    for entry in valid_signals:
   257→        concern_type = str(entry.get("type", "")).strip() or "design_concern"
   258→        file = str(entry.get("file", "")).strip() or "(unknown)"
   259→        by_type.setdefault(concern_type, []).append(file)
   260→
   261→    if not by_type:
   262→        return []
   263→
   264→    lines = [f"Overview ({len(valid_signals)} signals):"]
   265→    for concern_type, files in sorted(by_type.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1])):
   266→        if len(files) <= 3:
   267→            file_list = ", ".join(files)
   268→            lines.append(f"  {concern_type}: {len(files)} — {file_list}")
   269→        else:
   270→            sample = ", ".join(files[:2])
   271→            lines.append(f"  {concern_type}: {len(files)} — {sample}, ...")
   272→    lines.append("")
   273→    return lines
   274→
   275→
   276→def render_mechanical_concern_signals(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:
   277→    """Render mechanically-generated concern hypotheses for this batch."""
   278→    signals = batch.get("concern_signals")
   279→    if not isinstance(signals, list) or not signals:
   280→        return ""
   281→
   282→    valid_signals = _iter_valid_concern_signals(signals)
   283→    if not valid_signals:
   284→        return ""
   285→
   286→    lines: list[str] = []
   287→    lines.append("Mechanical concern signals — investigate and adjudicate:")
   288→    lines.extend(_build_concern_summary(valid_signals))
   289→    lines.append("For each concern, read the source code and report your verdict in issues[]:")
   290→    lines.append(
   291→        '  - Confirm → full issue object with concern_verdict: "confirmed"'
   292→    )
   293→    lines.append(
   294→        '  - Dismiss → minimal object: {concern_verdict: "dismissed", concern_fingerprint: "<hash>"}'
   295→    )
   296→    lines.append(
   297→        "    (only these 2 fields required — add optional reasoning/concern_type/concern_file)"
   298→    )
   299→    lines.append(
   300→        "  - Unsure → skip it (will be re-evaluated next review)"
   301→    )
   302→    lines.append("")
   303→
   304→    capped_signals = valid_signals[:30]
   305→    for entry in capped_signals:
   306→        lines.extend(_concern_signal_lines(entry))
   307→
   308→    extra = max(0, len(valid_signals) - len(capped_signals))
   309→    if extra:
   310→        lines.append(f"  (+{extra} more — use `desloppify show <detector> --no-budget` to explore)")
   311→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   312→
   313→
   314→def _coerce_finding_counts(raw: object) -> dict[str, int]:
   315→    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   316→        return {}
   317→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   318→    for det, count in raw.items():
   319→        if not isinstance(det, str):
   320→            continue
   321→        try:
   322→            normalized = int(count)
   323→        except (TypeError, ValueError):
   324→            continue
   325→        if normalized > 0:
   326→            counts[det] = normalized
   327→    return counts
   328→
   329→
   330→def render_findings_exploration_section(batch: PromptBatchPayload) -> str:
   331→    """Render CLI exploration commands for detector findings relevant to this batch."""
   332→    all_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   333→    for key in ("judgment_finding_counts", "mechanical_finding_counts"):
   334→        all_counts.update(_coerce_finding_counts(batch.get(key)))
   335→    if not all_counts:
   336→        return ""
   337→
   338→    lines = [
   339→        "RELEVANT FINDINGS — explore with CLI:",
   340→        "These detectors found patterns related to this dimension. Explore the findings,",
   341→        "then read the actual source code.",
   342→        "",
   343→    ]
   344→    for detector, n in sorted(all_counts.items()):
   345→        lines.append(f"  desloppify show {detector} --no-budget      # {n} findings")
   346→    lines.append("")
   347→    lines.append(
   348→        "Report actionable issues in issues[]. Use concern_verdict and concern_fingerprint"
   349→    )
   350→    lines.append("for findings you want to confirm or dismiss.")
   351→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   352→
   353→
   354→# Keep the old name as an alias so existing callers don't break.
   355→render_judgment_findings_section = render_findings_exploration_section
   356→
   357→
   358→def render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   359→    """Render workflow integrity checks for architecture/integration dimensions."""
   360→    if not dim_set.intersection(
   361→        {
   362→            "cross_module_architecture",
   363→            "high_level_elegance",
   364→            "mid_level_elegance",
   365→            "design_coherence",
   366→            "initialization_coupling",
   367→        }
   368→    ):
   369→        return ""
   370→    return (
   371→        "9j. Workflow integrity checks: when reviewing orchestration/queue/review flows,\n"
   372→        "    explicitly look for loop-prone patterns and blind spots:\n"
   373→        "    - repeated stale/reopen churn without clear exit criteria or gating,\n"
   374→        "    - packet/batch data being generated but dropped before prompt execution,\n"
   375→        "    - ranking/triage logic that can starve target-improving work,\n"
   376→        "    - reruns happening before existing open review work is drained.\n"
   377→        "    If found, propose concrete guardrails and where to implement them.\n"
   378→    )
   379→
   380→
   381→def render_package_org_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   382→    if "package_organization" not in dim_set:
   383→        return ""
   384→    return (
   385→        "9a. For package_organization, ground scoring in objective structure signals from "
   386→        "`holistic_context.structure` (root_files fan_in/fan_out roles, directory_profiles, "
   387→        "coupling_matrix). Prefer thresholded evidence (for example: fan_in < 5 for root "
   388→        "stragglers, import-affinity > 60%, directories > 10 files with mixed concerns).\n"
   389→        "9b. Suggestions must include a staged reorg plan (target folders, move order, "
   390→        "and import-update/validation commands).\n"
   391→        "9c. Also consult `holistic_context.structure.flat_dir_issues` for directories "
   392→        "flagged as overloaded, fragmented, or thin-wrapper patterns.\n"
   393→    )
   394→
   395→
   396→def render_abstraction_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   397→    if "abstraction_fitness" not in dim_set:
   398→        return ""
   399→    return (
   400→        "9d. For abstraction_fitness, use evidence from `holistic_context.abstractions`:\n"
   401→        "  - `delegation_heavy_classes`: classes where most methods forward to an inner "
   402→        "object — entries include class_name, delegate_target, sample_methods, and line number.\n"
   403→        "  - `facade_modules`: re-export-only modules with high re_export_ratio — entries "
   404→        "include samples (re-exported names) and loc.\n"
   405→        "  - `typed_dict_violations`: TypedDict fields accessed via .get()/.setdefault()/.pop() "
   406→        "— entries include typed_dict_name, violation_type, field, and line number.\n"
   407→        "  - `complexity_hotspots`: files where mechanical analysis found extreme parameter "
   408→        "counts, deep nesting, or disconnected responsibility clusters.\n"
   409→        "  Include `delegation_density`, `definition_directness`, and `type_discipline` "
   410→        "alongside existing sub-axes in dimension_notes when evidence supports it.\n"
   411→    )
   412→
   413→
   414→def render_dimension_focus(dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   415→    return (
   416→        render_package_org_focus(dim_set)
   417→        + render_abstraction_focus(dim_set)
   418→        + render_scan_evidence_focus(dim_set)
   419→        + render_workflow_integrity_focus(dim_set)
   420→    )
   421→
   422→
   423→def explode_to_single_dimension(
   424→    batches: list[PromptBatchPayload],
   425→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]] | None = None,
   426→) -> list[PromptBatchPayload]:
   427→    """Split multi-dimension batches into one batch per dimension.
   428→
   429→    Preserves seed files and rationale — each exploded batch keeps the same
   430→    file grouping but is scoped to a single dimension. When *dimension_prompts*
   431→    is provided, each exploded batch gets a public ``dimension_prompts`` map
   432→    scoped to its single dimension.
   433→    """
   434→    prompts = dimension_prompts or {}
   435→    result: list[PromptBatchPayload] = []
   436→    for batch in batches:
   437→        dims = batch.get("dimensions", [])
   438→        if not isinstance(dims, list):
   439→            result.append(batch)
   440→            continue
   441→        for dim in dims:
   442→            exploded: PromptBatchPayload = {**batch, "dimensions": [dim]}
   443→            dim_prompt = prompts.get(dim)
   444→            if isinstance(dim_prompt, dict):
   445→                exploded["dimension_prompts"] = {str(dim): dim_prompt}
   446→            result.append(exploded)
   447→    return result
   448→
   449→
   450→def render_dimension_prompts_block(
   451→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   452→    dimension_prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   453→) -> str:
   454→    """Render inline dimension guidance so the reviewer sees the full rubric."""
   455→    if not dimensions or not dimension_prompts:
   456→        return ""
   457→    lines: list[str] = ["DIMENSION TO EVALUATE:\n"]
   458→    for dim in dimensions:
   459→        prompt = dimension_prompts.get(dim)
   460→        if not isinstance(prompt, dict):
   461→            lines.append(f"## {dim}\n(no rubric available)\n")
   462→            continue
   463→        description = str(prompt.get("description", "")).strip()
   464→        lines.append(f"## {dim}")
   465→        if description:
   466→            lines.append(description)
   467→
   468→        look_for = prompt.get("look_for")
   469→        if isinstance(look_for, list) and look_for:
   470→            lines.append("Look for:")
   471→            for item in look_for:
   472→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   473→
   474→        skip = prompt.get("skip")
   475→        if isinstance(skip, list) and skip:
   476→            lines.append("Skip:")
   477→            for item in skip:
   478→                lines.append(f"- {item}")
   479→        lines.append("")
   480→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
   481→
   482→
   483→def render_dimension_context_block(
   484→    dimensions: tuple[str, ...],
   485→    dimension_contexts: dict[str, dict],
   486→) -> str:
   487→    """Render accumulated codebase context for dimensions that have insights.
   488→
   489→    Only surfaces headers in the prompt text — full descriptions are in the
   490→    blind packet's ``dimension_contexts`` section.
   491→    """
   492→    if not dimensions or not dimension_contexts:
   493→        return ""
   494→
   495→    sections: list[str] = []
   496→    for dim in dimensions:
   497→        ctx = dimension_contexts.get(dim)
   498→        if not isinstance(ctx, dict):
   499→            continue
   500→        insights = ctx.get("insights")
   501→        if not isinstance(insights, list) or not insights:
   502→            continue
   503→        lines: list[str] = [f"### {dim}"]
   504→        for insight in insights:
   505→            if not isinstance(insight, dict):
   506→                continue
   507→            header = str(insight.get("header", "")).strip()
   508→            if not header:
   509→                continue
   510→            settled = insight.get("settled", False)
   511→            positive = insight.get("positive", False)
   512→            tags = []
   513→            if settled:
   514→                tags.append("settled")
   515→            if positive:
   516→                tags.append("+")
   517→            prefix = f"[{', '.join(tags)}] " if tags else ""
   518→            lines.append(f"- {prefix}{header}")
   519→        if len(lines) > 1:
   520→            sections.append("\n".join(lines))
   521→
   522→    if not sections:
   523→        return ""
   524→
   525→    header_block = (
   526→        "## Codebase Characteristics\n\n"
   527→        "Previous reviews established these characteristics. [+] marks positive\n"
   528→        "patterns; [settled] means confirmed. Augment this list: add new\n"
   529→        "observations, refine descriptions, settle or remove items as needed.\n"
   530→        "For full details, read `dimension_contexts.{dimension}.insights`.\n\n"
   531→    )
   532→    footer = (
   533→        "\nPrinciples: Keep your own context updates succinct. Each insight should have\n"
   534→        "a clear header (5-10 words) and a description explaining WHY, not WHAT.\n"
   535→        "Positive patterns get `positive: true`. Settle items when confident.\n\n"
   536→    )
   537→    return header_block + "\n\n".join(sections) + footer
   538→
   539→
   540→def render_scoring_frame() -> str:
   541→    return (
   542→        "YOUR TASK: Read the code for this batch's dimension. Judge "
   543→        "how well the codebase serves a developer from that perspective. The dimension "
   544→        "rubric above defines what good looks like. "
   545→        "Cite specific observations that explain your judgment.\n\n"
   546→    )
   547→
   548→
   549→def render_scan_evidence_note() -> str:
   550→    return (
   551→        "Mechanical scan evidence — navigation aid, not scoring evidence:\n"
   552→        "The blind packet contains `holistic_context.scan_evidence` with aggregated signals "
   553→        "from all mechanical detectors — including complexity hotspots, error hotspots, signal "
   554→        "density index, boundary violations, and systemic patterns. Use these as starting "
   555→        "points for where to look beyond the seed files.\n\n"
   556→    )
   557→
   558→
   559→def render_task_requirements(*, issues_cap: int, dim_set: set[str]) -> str:
   560→    dim_focus = render_dimension_focus(dim_set)
   561→    lines = [
   562→        "Phase 1 — Observe:",
   563→        "1. Read the blind packet's `system_prompt` — scoring rules and calibration.",
   564→        "2. Study the dimension rubric (description, look_for, skip).",
   565→        "3. Review the existing characteristics list — which are settled? Which are positive? What needs updating?",
   566→        "4. Explore the codebase freely. Use scan evidence, historical issues, and mechanical findings as navigation aids.",
   567→        "5. Adjudicate mechanical concern signals (confirm/dismiss with fingerprint).",
   568→        "6. Augment the characteristics list via context_updates: positive patterns (positive: true), neutral characteristics, design insights.",
   569→        "7. Collect defects for issues[].",
   570→        "8. Respect scope controls: exclude files/directories marked by `exclude`, `suppress`, or non-production zone overrides.",
   571→        "9. Output a Phase 1 summary: list ALL characteristics for this dimension (existing + new, mark [+] for positive) and all defects collected. This is your consolidated reference for Phase 2.",
   572→        "",
   573→        "Phase 2 — Judge (after Phase 1 is complete):",
   574→        "10. Keep issues and scoring scoped to this batch's dimension.",
   575→        f"11. Return 0-{issues_cap} issues for this batch (empty array allowed).",
   576→    ]
   577→    next_num = 12
   578→    if dim_focus:
   579→        for focus_line in dim_focus.rstrip("\n").split("\n"):
   580→            lines.append(f"{next_num}. {focus_line.lstrip('0123456789abcdefghij. ')}")
   581→            next_num += 1
   582→    lines.append(
   583→        f"{next_num}. Complete `dimension_judgment`: write dimension_character "
   584→        "(synthesizing characteristics and defects) then score_rationale. "
   585→        "Set the score LAST."
   586→    )
   587→    next_num += 1
   588→    lines.append(
   589→        f"{next_num}. Output context_updates with your Phase 1 observations. "
   590→        "Use `add` with a clear header (5-10 words) and description (1-3 "
   591→        "sentences focused on WHY, not WHAT). Positive patterns get "
   592→        "`positive: true`. New insights can be `settled: true` when confident. "
   593→        "Use `settle` to promote existing unsettled insights. Use `remove` for "
   594→        "insights no longer true. Omit context_updates if no changes."
   595→    )
   596→    next_num += 1
   597→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Do not edit repository files.")
   598→    next_num += 1
   599→    lines.append(f"{next_num}. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences.")
   600→    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n\n"
   601→
   602→
   603→def render_scope_enums() -> str:
   604→    return (
   605→        "Scope enums:\n"
   606→        '- impact_scope: "local" | "module" | "subsystem" | "codebase"\n'
   607→        '- fix_scope: "single_edit" | "multi_file_refactor" | "architectural_change"\n\n'
   608→    )
   609→
   610→
   611→def join_non_empty_sections(*sections: str) -> str:
   612→    return "".join(section for section in sections if section)
   613→
```
